# Results

Plugin v2.6.0-rc2 on lab dev 98899a0. This run is on the lab as it stood before the v0.3.0 fixes; none of them was in it, and it is not a run of v0.3.0. The first run on a v0.3.0 tree is planned for v0.4.0 (#104).

Fourteen cells, -j 4 with a 60 s stagger, 2026-10-13. Wall time 16 h 10 min; peak host load 13.1, at most 10 domains running at once.

Totals (thirteen cells with verdicts, 3761 rows): 1683 PASS, 1384 N/A, 401 FAIL, 293 BLOCKED. One cell (chr) wrote no verdicts: its base image could not be built on the lab host (`qemu-img: Unknown protocol 'fetch-base-image'`), a lab defect. Each cell's retry pass is part of its result (see below). Capture hygiene was not tallied for this run.

Read the FAIL and BLOCKED rows with four things in mind.

- The lab and the plugin both changed since the previous page (lab 7fc4ee1 to 98899a0, plugin rc1 to rc2). A row that changed between the two pages cannot be put down to the plugin alone.
- Three cells are lab-side defects, not plugin findings. kea-relay: 0 PASS because the relay VM has no `sysctl`, so 148 rows read BLOCKED ("relay: read ip_forward ... sysctl: command not found"); the relay probe itself passed this time. openwrt: most of its 117 FAIL rows come from the lab's source probe ("ping: permission denied") or the source not reaching the address. pihole: the bridge shape stopped at a firewall-rule check over ssh, the A1 container image pull (`alpine:3.20`) and the FTL restart limit ("start attempted too often") hit the other shapes. dnsmasq bridge-ipam: the shape stopped after A2 when the lab recreated its network and the host bridge or its `eth1` port did not come up after `netplan apply`, so its 60 remaining rows read (missing).
- 35 verdicts failed with CreateEndpoint hitting its client timeout before the retry pass (38 on the previous page); none do after it. 31 of the 35 turned PASS on the rerun; host-debian11-docker2010 macvlan D3c and openwrt bridge B1 stayed FAIL for other reasons, and two reruns (host-ubuntu2404 bridge-ipam F2a, macvlan D1c) never ran: the rerun aborted on a verdict file the lab itself wrote with a line break inside its `isolation:` line (host-ubuntu2404 macvlan-ipam A5), which `labctl` refuses to read. Those two rows read (missing) and their first-pass verdicts are in `host-ubuntu2404/retried/`. This page was rendered from a copy of the evidence in which that one line break was folded into a space; the tarball holds the original file.
- The D3, D4 and F rows now carry verdicts on bridge, macvlan and ipvlan; the two IPAM shapes are N/A for them by design.

Plugin findings:

- Parent busy (kea 61 rows, host-ubuntu2404-isc-dhcp 62, pihole 28): `failed to create macvlan link on "eth1": device or resource busy`, after one shape's scenarios on the same parent NIC. No CreateEndpoint timeout preceded the first busy row in any of the three cells. The lab's own parent-clean check read "clean" in the docker host's namespace just before it. What was left on the parent is not recorded: no `ip link` capture exists for the window, and the main-pass plugin journals of kea and host-ubuntu2404-isc-dhcp were overwritten by the retry pass. Probable cause, not confirmed: a macvlan or ipvlan child living in a container namespace, which the check cannot see; in kea and host-ubuntu2404-isc-dhcp the busy run starts right after a failed host-reboot scenario (A5) on macvlan-ipam.
- D4b (prefix withdrawn): FAIL on bridge and macvlan on kea, isc-dhcp and dnsmasq; the withdrawn prefix's address stays and counts down its valid lifetime. Same as the previous page. Not an open plugin defect: that judge expected the address to go, and the lab's judge since #102 treats an address that stays and counts down as the correct behaviour (plugin #1368). The row was not re-run with the new judge.
- C5 failover-primary-killed: FAIL on all five shapes of isc-failover. The client's unicast renewal does happen between T1 and T2, but to the other partner than the one the judge names (both partners answered the bound ACK). Probable cause, not confirmed: a judge defect, not a plugin one.
- D2 SLAAC: the container still sends a DHCPv6 Information-request and no Solicit; the row is PASS now because the judge accepts that. The plugin behaviour is unchanged.
- B7 lease release and C6b late squatter: PASS on bridge and macvlan for every source that has verdicts for them (FAIL on the previous page); the remaining FAIL rows are the busy cluster, B7 on isc-dhcp ipvlan (an address-less parent sends none) and the lab-side cells. The cause of the change is not isolated.
- NAT64 prefix (pref64): PASS on kea, isc-dhcp and dnsmasq bridge and macvlan. The health endpoint reports `fail` in those captures through the cumulative `address_conflicts` check from the C6 squatter scenarios; the pref64 scenario does not cause it.
